## Legend & conventions

- `—` — value not reported in the source table.
- Units in parentheses (e.g. `(USD)`, `(Minutes)`) come from the source table headers.
- Benchmark descriptions are listed once in **Column notes** above instead of being repeated in every header.
- Negative numbers use a plain minus sign (e.g. `-12.5`).
